Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any homological module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 496. The projective resolution has length at most 704 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 496 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homological structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 704. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
